# A4 register: src/lib (minus agent, sources, obligations, workspace, community, propagation), src/workflows, src/stores, src/types, 2026-10-11

Lane audit2-a4-src-lib-2 (FACT lane, read-only, Sonnet). Tree: master `0d0766347` (HEAD, clean at start; `git ls-files` of the four paths minus the six lane-A3 directories = 504 tracked files, 107,887 lines; the same 504 as `find`). One file created under `fsi-app/scripts/tmp/audit2/`; no commit, push, PR, CI trigger or database access. Charter: `audit2-charter.md` read whole; CLAUDE.md rules 14 and 15 applied.

## READ DEPTH (charter method 1; stated before the rows)

The scope is 504 files (293 non-test, 211 test; 2.85 MB of non-test source, 1.77 MB of test source, plus a 14.5 MB fixture). I did NOT read every file whole. What was done, by file group:

- **22 non-test files read whole**: every file under src/lib/account, admin, api, applicability, auth, cache (all lines).
- **123 non-test files read with comment blocks over 3 lines shortened to their first 2 lines** (all code lines shown and read; long header comments NOT read): src/lib/classification, contracts (envelope, factor-tier, source-licence, verbatim-grounding fully for code; vocabularies.mjs read as an export list plus section heads), corrections, connections (except derive-tags.mjs), credibility, csv, and the small files of the other directories.
- **148 non-test files read as a structural digest only** (header comment, every export signature, every `.from()`/`.rpc()`/`process.env`/`fetch`/insert/upsert/delete line, plus all lines for files under 30 to 90 lines): the large and the data/constant files, including src/lib/supabase-server.ts (4,983 lines), data.ts (1,148), trust.ts (1,617), forward-events/extract-forward-events.mjs (1,945), intake/record-facts.mjs (1,094), and the files listed under "Unverified" below. For these, CALLED BY, CONSUMED BY, PROVEN BY and the prior-claim checks are executed facts; the WHAT sentence beyond signatures and I/O lines carries a [HYPOTHESIS] token.
- **211 test files: executed, not read.** Each was run once with `node --test <file>` from the repo root (credential env vars unset, `FSI_NO_ENV_FILE=1`): 211 files, 2508 tests, 2508 pass, 0 fail, 0 skipped, 0 todo, every exit code 0 (per-file results kept in the scratchpad, summarised in the PROVEN BY column). Assertion quality (vacuity) was NOT assessed. Test names appear in the run output but the bodies were not read.
- **Files NOT read at all**: `src/lib/connections/fixtures/census-rows.apply-ready.json` (14.5 MB, 20,562 lines; existence, referrers and size checked only), and the three forward-events fixtures (existence and referrers only).

Mechanical evidence behind the CALLED BY column: a Node script parsed every `import`/`require`/`import()` specifier of every tracked .ts/.tsx/.mjs/.js/.cjs file in fsi-app/, .github/, .claude/ and the repo root (5,381 tracked files, text files under 3 MB), resolved relative and `@/` specifiers to tracked paths, and recorded importer file:line. Dynamic string paths (readdir, readFileSync of a computed path) are NOT import edges; where they matter the row says so. Name-based export checks (`exports.mjs`) treat any word-boundary mention anywhere as a use, so zero-reference results are certain and non-zero results are an upper bound.

Test wiring (CI): the repo now has `ci.yml` (jobs inputs, static, schema, app, master). [CONFIRMED: `gh run view 38124942168` (ci.yml, pull_request, 2026-10-11, success)] job `static` runs "Run discipline test suite" (step 10), "Run fitness functions" (step 18) and "App unit tests requiring npm deps (*.npmtest.mjs)" (step 21), all success. [CONFIRMED: `gh run view 38124930467` (ci.yml, push to master, 0d0766347, success)] on a push to master the jobs `static`, `schema` and `app` are SKIPPED and only `master` runs, so no unit test of this scope ran on the master push; they run on PR runs and in the pre-push hook. Test discovery: `node fsi-app/.discipline/lib/test-discovery.mjs` returned 771 files; every in-scope test is discovered or is a `.npmtest.mjs` (run by step 21) except `src/lib/trust.selftest.mjs`, which is run by the F10 fitness sentinel (F10-source-credibility-syndication.mjs:19), not by the suite.

Last real run (charter column): the only runtime evidence available without database access is the committed ledger snapshot `fsi-app/.discipline/governance/harness-ledger-export.json` (captured 2026-10-10, 280 rows, 23 families). Every family I mapped to a module here has its latest run `dry`, `plan` or `forced_dry` (apply/execute configs exist for mint, ledger-consume, downstream-chain run-001, fetch-drain run-006, brief-apply run-001; the latest mint run `mint-run-004` carries config execute with trigger workflow_run_forced_dry). No runtime apply run after the build-mode hold appears. A module with no harness family and no test shows NEVER in LAST REAL RUN.

## SUMMARY

Rows: 504 (293 non-test objects including 19 data/fixture files, 211 test files).

### Counts per STATUS

| STATUS | all rows | non-test | test |
|---|---|---|---|
| LIVE | 472 | 261 | 211 |
| HALF-SLICE | 3 | 3 | 0 |
| DEAD | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| UNPROVEN | 28 | 28 | 0 |
| UNWIRED | 1 | 1 | 0 |
| STALE | 0 | 0 | 0 |

UNPROVEN here means: live module of 40 or more lines with a capability, learning or self-maintenance role and NO test file importing it or sitting beside it in the repo (28 rows). It may still be exercised by its callers, a fitness function or the rendering guard; this lane did not trace those.

### Counts per CLASS

| CLASS | rows | of which non-test |
|---|---|---|
| CAPABILITY | 139 | 139 |
| LEARNING | 51 | 51 |
| SELF-MAINTENANCE | 279 | 68 |
| NONE | 35 | 35 |

NONE = UI plumbing, shared types, hooks and stores that carry no data-quality or learning role (35 rows). Test files are all SELF-MAINTENANCE. Classes for non-test rows are my reading of the module header and callers, not a measured property.

### DISAGREES with prior audits (newest prior first; each is a row in Prior findings)

1. `lib/entities/resolve.mjs`: aud-sys-1 F-01 (2026-10-11) lists it among the BUILT entity-spine files; its alias writer (`writeEntityAliases`) and reader (`resolveEntityByAlias`) have no caller in src, scripts, .discipline or workflows except `scripts/verify/surface-acceptance.mjs` and tests [CONFIRMED: git grep]. Status HALF-SLICE.
2. `lib/trust.ts`: dead-code-census 2026-10-08 does not list `createDefaultTrustMetrics` and `computeBaselineTrustScore`; both have zero references anywhere in src, scripts or .discipline [CONFIRMED: git grep -> no output]. `evaluateProvisionalSource` is referenced only by `src/lib/trust-evaluators.npmtest.mjs`.
3. `lib/api/org.ts` F2-1a, `lib/connections/resource-lookup.ts` F2-1d, `lib/forward-events/read-upcoming.mjs` F2-1e, `lib/tags/server.ts` F2-1j, `stores/settingsStore.ts` F2-1k/A3B-04, `workflows/generate-brief.ts` A3B-03, `lib/supabase-server.ts` F2-1i: app-audit-a3 / a3b (2026-09-30) findings are STILL-OPEN; the supabase-server count went from 8 to 13 sites [CONFIRMED: `grep -cE "const \{ data(: *[A-Za-z_]+)? \} = await" -> 13`].
4. `lib/trust.ts` A3B-06 (908 lines) is now 1,617 lines.
5. FIXED-SINCE (prior claim no longer true): F25-1 chip-selection (#944), F2-1b provision-personal-workspace (#960 AUTH-2), A3B-05 scoring.sortResources and L11 PRIORITY_DISPLAY_LABEL (#1087 DEAD-1c), L12 SERIES_ITEM_MAP empty (#584), L11 coverage/identity test-only (#648), F25-2/F25-4/F25-6 census-writer, program-total, corridor-id (deleted by #1007 DEAD-1), L11 dashboard/credibility.ts and critical-items.ts (deleted by #507), L12 export/download.ts (deleted by #501), a3b operations/automate-vs-hire.mjs (deleted by #922).
6. REFUTED: F-CON-3 (derive-tags fs read at import taking down a live route): no importer under src/app or src/components; L11 "d3/hooks.selftest not globbed": test-discovery includes it.

### Unverified (rows carrying a [HYPOTHESIS] token; counts, then detail at the end of the file)

- 148 non-test rows: WHAT read from a structural digest, not the whole file.
- 123 non-test rows: header comment blocks over 3 lines were not read (code was).
- 211 test rows: executed and counted, bodies not read, vacuity not assessed.
- `connections/fixtures/census-rows.apply-ready.json` and 3 forward-events fixtures: not read.
- F-INT-2 (a3c, unconditional agent_run_searches read at mint, latency) [HYPOTHESIS]: not evaluated, needs production latency.
- Every LAST REAL RUN cell for a runtime-attached module rests on the committed ledger snapshot of 2026-10-10, not on a live read.
- Which UNPROVEN modules are exercised by a fitness function, a script test outside src or the rendering guard: not traced.

### The ten rows that matter most (no adjectives)

1. `lib/supabase-server.ts`: 4,983 lines, 43 exports, 13 `{ data }` reads that drop `error` (a3 F2-1i counted 8); read as a digest only.
2. `lib/llm/haiku-classify.ts`: calls the Anthropic SDK directly (line 158), outside spend-client; the F15 allowlist entry is unchanged since a3 (2026-09-30).
3. `lib/entities/resolve.mjs`: alias and hierarchy reader/writer (migration 377) with no runtime caller; the learning emitting-table `entity_aliases.json` watches a table nothing writes at runtime.
4. `lib/email/send-invitation-email.ts`: returns `{ delivered: false, configured: false }` unconditionally; the invitations route calls it.
5. `lib/intake/flywheel-defect.ts` + `lib/intake/mint-item.ts`: the rule-16(d) defect recorder and three other inserts discard failures with `.then(() => {}, () => {})` (lines 79; 309, 448, 470).
6. `lib/classification/routing.mjs`: `routeItemBySourceAxis5` and `isAnomalousCategory` have no production caller; `detectDrift` and `observedDistributionFromItems` are called by the classification scripts.
7. `lib/llm/metered-gate.mjs`: zero production importers (F25 allowlisted since 2026-08-31); its 20-test file passes.
8. `lib/trust.ts`: 1,617 lines; `createDefaultTrustMetrics` and `computeBaselineTrustScore` have zero references; `evaluateProvisionalSource` is test-only.
9. `workflows/generate-brief.ts`: `groundStep` loadItem/loadClaims drop `error` (lines 236, 240) while `eraseStep` in the same file checks every error.
10. `lib/api/org.ts`, `connections/resource-lookup.ts`, `tags/server.ts`, `forward-events/read-upcoming.mjs`, `stores/settingsStore.ts`: the same dropped-error read class as a3 F2-1, still present at the lines cited in Prior findings.
